- **planroom-proposer**: Writes a submitted Planroom write-up into openspec/changes/&lt;change-id&gt;/ as proposal, specs, design and tasks that pass openspec validate, or for a Markdown plan into &lt;change-id&gt;.md in its plan folder (agent-plans/&lt;change-id&gt;/ by default). Only the planroom skill delegates to it, on phase.submit. ([MosesWescombe/planroom](https://github.com/MosesWescombe/planroom/blob/HEAD/agents/planroom-proposer.md) · ★2)
- **planroom-researcher**: Read-only research for a Planroom plan, ask or review - answers one factual question about this repo or a review's worktree, with path:line evidence, or about an outside product, from its docs, release notes and pricing pages. Only the planroom, planroom-ask and planroom-review skills delegate to it; not for planning, design or edits. ([MosesWescombe/planroom](https://github.com/MosesWescombe/planroom/blob/HEAD/agents/planroom-researcher.md) · ★2)
- **planroom-reviewer**: Reviews one dimension of a change for a Planroom Review (correctness, security, performance, design, tests or plans) or all of them, or in verify mode tries to refute another reviewer's findings and keeps only what the code confirms. Reads the review's worktree only. Only the planroom-review skill delegates to it. ([MosesWescombe/planroom](https://github.com/MosesWescombe/planroom/blob/HEAD/agents/planroom-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **plans**: `rimz accounts --json`, `accounts list --json`, and `accounts show … --json` expose each login's plan identity and fixed weekly capacity. These fields are exposure only: no launch, routing, default switch, or score reads them, and RimZ never derives remaining units from them. The [JSON reference](../../reference/cli/accounts.md#json-output) defines the row contract. The [provider model](./provider ([rimio-ai/rimz](https://github.com/rimio-ai/rimz/blob/HEAD/docs/internals/agents/plans.md) · ★36)
- **plans**: - Every plan, spec, and regroup produces two files with the same stem. - `&lt;name&gt;.md` is for agents: full detail, plus the todo.txt section. See [todo-txt.md](todo-txt.md). - `&lt;name&gt;.human.md` is for the human: the same plan, short and visual. - When a request names the overview, resolve it to its full sibling (strip only the trailing `.human` segment immediately before the extension) and hand that ([edheltzel/GAP](https://github.com/edheltzel/GAP/blob/HEAD/global/docs/agents/plans.md) · ★2)
- **plans**: An agent that can act (pay, approve, file, ship) should not answer "done" in prose. It should answer with a **plan**: a sequence of operations over your model. GND checks that plan before anything runs. It verifies each step's preconditions, every intermediate state, and the **frame**: no fact may change unless a declared operation changes it. ([vasiloglou/gnd-public](https://github.com/vasiloglou/gnd-public/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/plans.md) · ★1)
- **plans**: How a plan in `docs/superpowers/plans/` marks the text each edit replaces, how those anchors are checked before the edit runs, how a task's diff is reviewed, and how a plan's workspace closes. ([eranroseman/agent-plugins](https://github.com/eranroseman/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/plans.md))
- **planstep-runner**: Runs and supervises RoslynSentinel.Tools.PlanStepRunner (via roslynsentinel-planstep.ps1) end-to-end, dispatching model-eval-log-analyst / blocker-writer / worktree-diff-sentinel as needed. Use for driving or reviewing a PlanStepRunner run against a real model, one or more plan steps at a time. ([sinthor505/RoslynSentinel](https://github.com/sinthor505/RoslynSentinel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/planstep-runner.md) · ★1)
